<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着事件视界望远镜等先进望远镜的出现，黑洞阴影的直接成像成为现实，而精确的数值模拟对于重现黑洞外观及约束其物理参数至关重要。本工作旨在提供一个简单但可靠的框架来模拟Schwarzschild黑洞图像。

Method: 采用光线追踪算法，通过四阶Runge-Kutta方案对光子测地线方程进行积分以生成图像；模型中考虑了旋转吸积盘引起的引力红移和Doppler红移效应；用收敛性测试验证数值精度，并讨论弱场近似的有效性。

Result: 数值方法通过收敛性测试验证了精度；生成的模拟图像与EHT拍摄的M87*图像在定性上具有可比性，表明该框架能合理再现黑洞阴影的基本特征。

Conclusion: 该框架为模拟Schwarzschild黑洞与薄旋转吸积盘系统的图像提供了一个简单有效的工具，适用于黑洞阴影的再现和物理参数约束研究。

Abstract: With the arrival of advanced telescopes such as the Event Horizon Telescope (EHT), direct imaging of a black hole's shadow became a reality. Simulations play an important role in reproducing their appearance and constraining physical parameters of the black hole. We present a simple framework of simulating images of Schwarzschild black holes surrounded by thin, rotating accretion disks, accounting for gravitational and Doppler redshifts. A ray tracing algorithm based on integrating the photon geodesic equations with a 4th-order Runge-Kutta scheme is used for imaging. The accuracy of the numerical method is verified by convergence tests. A brief discussion of the validity of the weak field approximation is included and a qualitative comparison between the simulated images and the EHT image of M87* is presented.

</details>


### [102] [Entropy-geometry correspondence as effective nonlocal gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07046)
*Kimet Jusufi,Ankit Anand*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文针对静态球对称引力，建立了熵-几何对应关系的算子形式体系。从广义熵出发，重构出有效的非局域形状因子、坐标空间源、累积质量轮廓及相应的时空几何，并系统分析了多种广义熵（包括Bekenstein-Hawking、Rényi、Tsallis-Cirto、Barrow、Kaniadakis、对数/指数修正熵及LQG熵）下重构解的物理一致性、红外/紫外行为，以及回复标准Schwarzschild描述的极限条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义熵修正（如Rényi、Tsallis等）广泛用于描述黑洞量子引力效应，但缺乏统一框架将熵修正与时空几何直接联系起来。作者希望建立熵-几何对应的算子表述，从而在统一的数学体系中理解广义熵如何决定非局域引力修正、有效质量和Newton耦合，并检验其物理自洽性。

Method: 提出算子形式的熵-几何对应框架：(1) 从广义熵出发构造有效的非局域形状因子；(2) 通过形状因子导出坐标空间源；(3) 由源计算出累积质量轮廓；(4) 由质量轮廓重建时空几何。关键结果：证明了广义熵精确复现面积定律dS=dA/4G_S(r_+)，其中G_S是随尺度变化的Newton耦合；同时推导了重构视界成为具有正温度的事件视界的条件。

Result: 建成了广义熵与非局域引力光晕、尺度依赖有效质量/Newton耦合之间的直接算子对应关系；分析了各熵形式对应的重构源的IR/UV行为；给出标准Schwarzschild解恢复的极限条件；证明了广义熵与重构的跑动Newton耦合满足面积定律，保证了热力学一致性；指出由于视界熵由作用量决定，熵修正更应归于引力扇区而非物质扇区。

Conclusion: 该算子框架为广义熵与引力几何之间提供了统一的构造性对应，既保证了热力学一致性（面积定律精确成立），也界定了物理上可接受的熵修正形式及其适用条件，暗示熵修正的动力学根源应位于引力的非局域结构中。

Abstract: We develop an operator formulation of the entropy-geometry correspondence for static, spherically symmetric gravity. Starting from a generalized entropy, we reconstruct an effective nonlocal form factor, its coordinate-space source, the associated cumulative mass profile, and the resulting spacetime geometry. The construction is worked out for the Bekenstein-Hawking, Rényi, Tsallis-Cirto, Barrow, Kaniadakis entropies, logarithmically/exponentially corrected entropy and LQG inspired entropy. The operator representation provides a direct relation between generalized entropy, nonlocal gravitational dressing, and a scale-dependent effective mass or Newton coupling. We analyze the reconstructed sources and their infrared and ultraviolet behavior, discuss their physical consistency, and identify the limits in which the standard Schwarzschild description is recovered. We show that the generalized entropy exactly reproduces the area law with the reconstructed running Newton coupling, $\dd S=\dd A/4G_S(r_+)$, ensuring thermodynamic consistency. Since horizon entropy is determined by the action, this favors entropy corrections in the gravitational sector. We also derive the conditions for the reconstructed horizon to be an event horizon with positive temperature.

</details>


### [103] [Full analytic expressions of overlap reduction functions for anisotropies of the stochastic gravitational-wave background with pulsar timing arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07095)
*Kun Zhou,Jin Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文严格推导了脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）中所有六种偏振模式的全解析各向异性重叠缩减函数（ORF）表达式，通过变量替换和坐标旋转解决了标量纵模和矢量模中的发散问题，建立了无近似、可计算至任意阶的通用框架，为各向异性天空映射、偏振模式分离和新物理搜寻提供了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PTA数据分析中使用的重叠缩减函数计算基于短波长近似，该近似对标量纵模失效，且无法处理频率依赖性和各向异性情形。这限制了PTA对引力理论检验、超质量黑洞研究及早期宇宙物理探测的能力，亟需一个严格、无近似的解析计算框架。

Method: 该工作从解析交叉关联框架出发严格推导完整的响应函数。首先揭示了所有偏振模式中各向异性ORF积分之间的内在对称性及其相互关系；然后通过变量替换和坐标旋转将复杂的多重积分转化为可处理的形式，并解决了矢量和标量纵模中的发散问题；最终建立了可生成任意阶全解析各向异性ORF表达式的通用框架。

Result: 建立了适用于全部六种偏振模式的通用解析框架，给出了直至l≤5的完整表达式。该框架完全无近似，其(0,0)分量可精确恢复到各向同性的Hellings-Downs曲线。相较于数值积分方法，该解析结果具有更广泛的适用范围、更快的计算速度和更高的精度。

Conclusion: 该通用解析框架为PTA数据的各向异性天空映射、偏振模式分离以及新物理搜寻提供了坚实的理论基础和高效的计算工具，显著提升了PTA数据分析的能力和精度。

Abstract: Pulsar timing arrays (PTAs) have detected a stochastic gravitational-wave background (SGWB) in the nanohertz band, enabling tests of gravity and cosmology, as well as studies of supermassive black holes and early Universe physics. PTA data analysis relies on cross-correlating timing residuals, where overlap reduction functions (ORFs) critically determine sensitivity. Conventional ORF calculations using the short-wavelength approximation break down for the scalar longitudinal mode and cannot handle frequency dependence or anisotropies. This work rigorously derives the full response functions within an analytical cross-correlation framework. We reveal, for the first time, intrinsic symmetries and relations among anisotropic ORF integrals for all polarizations. By variable substitutions and coordinate rotations, we transform complex integrals into tractable forms, resolving divergences in vector and scalar longitudinal modes. Building on this, we establish a universal framework yielding fully analytical expressions for anisotropic ORFs to arbitrary order for all six modes. As a direct application, we give complete expressions up to l <= 5. This framework is free of approximations; its (0,0) component recovers the isotropic Hellings--Downs curve. Compared with numerical integration, our results offer broader applicability, faster computation, and higher precision, providing a valuable foundation for anisotropic sky mapping, polarization-mode separation, and new-physics searches with PTA data.

</details>


### [104] [Colored Black Holes in Logarithmic Nonlinear Yang--Mills Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07150)
*Celio R. Muniz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在四维爱因斯坦引力耦合SU(2)对数非线性Yang-Mills场框架下，构造了静态、球对称、渐近平坦的带色黑洞。与基于Wu-Yang ansatz的解不同，该解的规范扇区包含动态径向振幅，源于真正的非线性耦合边值问题。数值构建了基本单节点分支，验证了其在线性极限下收敛于普通Einstein-Yang-Mills带色黑洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非线性Yang-Mills理论中带色黑洞的全新解族，特别是超越Wu-Yang ansatz限制的具有动态径向振幅的构型，以理解非线性效应对黑洞性质的影响。

Method: 通过积分恒等式排除无节点的符号确定解，证明带色构型必须具有节点结构；采用数值方法求解耦合非线性边值问题，构建基本单节点分支，并系统研究非线性参数对黑洞全局性质的影响。

Result: 增加对数非线性会降低ADM质量、提高Hawking温度、将规范场节点推向更大半径；在所研究参数范围内未检测到分支终止，外部几何在固定径向域上趋近Schwarzschild，而色结构发展为更长的尾部；事件视界内无Cauchy视界，趋于Schwarzschild型类空曲率奇点，有限极限质量且无振荡质量膨胀；Hawking温度拐点导致热容发散和变号，表明局部热力学稳定性转变。

Conclusion: 线性理论的恢复在中心附近是非均匀的：外部近乎线性的解在深内部最终进入强对数区域。该工作揭示了非线性Yang-Mills作用对带色黑洞结构、热力学和稳定性性质的深刻影响。

Abstract: We construct static, spherically symmetric, and asymptotically flat colored black holes in four-dimensional Einstein gravity coupled to a logarithmic nonlinear Yang--Mills field with gauge group SU(2). Unlike solutions based on the Wu--Yang ansatz, the gauge sector contains a dynamical radial amplitude, and the configurations arise from a genuinely coupled nonlinear boundary-value problem. An integral identity excludes nontrivial sign-definite solutions approaching a magnetically neutral Yang--Mills vacuum, implying that colored configurations must be nodal. We numerically construct the fundamental one-node branch and verify its convergence to the ordinary Einstein--Yang--Mills colored black hole in the linear limit. Increasing the logarithmic nonlinearity lowers the ADM mass, raises the Hawking temperature, and displaces the gauge-field node toward larger radii. Branch termination is not detected over the full range of parameters investigated. Instead, the exterior geometry approaches Schwarzschild on fixed radial domains, while the colored structure develops an increasingly extended tail. Although no independent asymptotic Yang--Mills charge is present, the non-Abelian hair leaves a subleading imprint on the far-field geometry. Inside the event horizon, the representative nonlinear solutions possess no Cauchy horizon and approach a Schwarzschild-type spacelike curvature singularity, with a finite limiting mass and no oscillary mass inflation. The recovery of the linear theory is therefore nonuniform near the cetonter: solutions that are nearly linear in the exterior eventually enter the strongly logarithmic regime in the deep interior. Finally, turning points of the Hawking temperature produce divergences and sign changes in heat capacity, indicating transitions of local thermodynamic stability.

</details>


### [105] [Quantum-cosmological corrections to inflationary spectra from Gaussian wave packets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07164)
*Sebastien Sonet,Alessandro Tronconi,Giovanni Venturi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在量子宇宙学背景下，研究了均匀暴胀-引力系统与微扰耦合的Wheeler-DeWitt方程解中的量子纠缠。通过将均匀暴胀变量边缘化，获得了暴胀谱的解析表达式，发现与标准半经典谱的微小偏差显著大于通常考虑的量子引力修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子宇宙学中，描述均匀自由度的波函数与描述微扰的波函数之间存在量子纠缠，这种纠缠可能对暴胀谱产生可观测效应，但此前尚未被充分研究。

Method: 研究了两个简化模型，它们均具有未来德西特吸引子。在忽略非绝热效应和Mukhanov-Sasaki方程中O(ħ²)阶贡献的条件下，通过边缘化均匀暴胀变量，获得暴胀谱的解析表达式，并在可靠的近似区域内进行数值评估。

Result: 产生了与标准半经典谱的微小偏差，且这些偏差显著大于该背景下通常考虑的量子引力修正。

Conclusion: 均匀自由度与微扰之间的量子纠缠可能在暴胀谱中留下可观测信号，其效应不可忽略，值得进一步研究。

Abstract: In the context of quantum cosmology, we study quantum entanglement in solutions of the Wheeler--DeWitt equation for a homogeneous inflaton--gravity system coupled to perturbations. The wave function describing the homogeneous degrees of freedom is entangled with the wave function describing the perturbations, and this may lead to potentially observable effects on the inflationary spectra. We restrict our analysis to two simplified models that admit a future de Sitter attractor, while neglecting non-adiabatic effects and contributions of order $\mathcal{O}(\hbar^2)$ in the Mukhanov--Sasaki equation. Analytical expressions for the resulting spectra are obtained by marginalising over the homogeneous inflaton variable and can be evaluated in regimes where reliable approximations are available. Small deviations from the standard semiclassical spectra are indeed generated, and these are significantly larger than the quantum-gravitational corrections usually considered in this context.

</details>


### [106] [Anomaly quenching and dynamical cooling of Hawking evaporation in Horndeski gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07182)
*Amogh Srivastav,S. Shankaranarayanan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究一阶Horndeski标量-张量耦合对二维CGHS黑洞蒸发过程的影响，发现表面引力与霍金温度随黑洞质量损失而动态降低，蒸发可能在微扰边界处停止并留下稳定的宏观冷残余，同时细粒度纠缠熵从线性增长过渡到渐近对数行为，暗示量子信息可能永久保留在残余中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解修改引力理论下黑洞蒸发过程的半经典行为，以及量子信息是否可能在无事件视界正则性破坏的情况下被保留。

Method: 对CGHS黑洞施加一阶Horndeski标量-张量耦合扰动，通过精确标量曲率不变量分析坐标奇异性，结合共形迹反常与Robinson-Wilczek引力反常计算渐近辐射通量，并用重求和温度方程外推蒸发终止行为。

Result: 在γ₃ΦX线性耦合扇区，表面引力和霍金温度随质量损失动态下降；蒸发可能停止并在微扰控制边界留下稳定宏观冷残余；渐近辐射通量精确反映几何偏移；细粒度纠缠熵从线性增长过渡到渐近对数增长，偏离标准半经典Page曲线。

Conclusion: 量子信息可能永久保留在冷残余中，且无需破坏事件视界正则性即可改变标准的半经典Page曲线，为理解黑洞信息疑难提供了新的理论途径。

Abstract: We investigate the semiclassical Hawking evaporation of a two-dimensional Callan-Giddings-Harvey-Strominger (CGHS) black hole perturbed by first-order Horndeski scalar-tensor couplings. By evaluating the exact scalar curvature invariants, we demonstrate that coordinate singularities near the apparent horizon parametrize a physical deformation of the surface gravity while preserving a strictly regular event horizon. In the linear-order $γ_3 ΦX$ coupling sector, the surface gravity and correspondingly the Hawking temperature decreases dynamically as the black hole loses mass. Extrapolating this cooling effect via a resummed temperature equation suggests that evaporation halts, potentially leaving behind a stable, macroscopic cold remnant at the boundary of perturbative control. Utilizing both the conformal trace anomaly and the Robinson-Wilczek gravitational anomaly, we establish that the asymptotic radiation flux identically reflects this geometric shift. Consequently, the fine-grained entanglement entropy of the Hawking radiation departs from linear growth and transitions to an asymptotic logarithmic regime. This indicates that quantum information may be permanently retained within the remnant, altering the standard semiclassical Page curve without requiring a breakdown of horizon regularity.

</details>


### [107] [New Universal Relations for Magnetized Neutron Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07172)
*Byon N. Jayawiguna,Siddarth Ajith,Takuya Katagiri,Kent Yagi,Piyabut Burikham*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探索了磁化中子星的新的普适关系（universal relations），聚焦于纯极向偶极磁场构型，将磁场效应微扰处理到二阶，发现了磁偶极矩与恒星致密性之间的精确普适关系，以及磁偶极矩、磁致四极矩和椭率之间的近似普适关系（方程状态引起的分数变化约O(10%)），并用牛顿多方球的解析估计佐证了数值结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与中子星质量-半径关系对核物质状态方程敏感不同，某些中子星性质满足对状态方程仅有微弱依赖的近似普适关系。本文旨在为磁化中子星探索新的普适关系，此前这方面的研究尚属空白。

Method: 采用纯极向偶极磁场构型，将磁场效应作为场强的二阶微扰处理；通过恒星质量和极点磁场强度对相关物理量进行适当归一化；对多种状态方程进行数值求解以验证普适性；并利用牛顿多方球进行解析估计以支持数值观察。

Result: 发现了磁偶极矩与恒星致密性之间的精确普适关系；发现了磁偶极矩、磁致四极矩和椭率之间的近似普适关系，其状态方程引起的分数变化约为O(10%)；解析估计成功再现了数值行为。

Conclusion: 新发现的磁偶极矩与四极矩之间的普适关系对包含磁化中子星的双星系统的引力波信号分析可能尤为有用，为这类信号的理论建模提供了新的工具。

Abstract: Unlike the neutron-star mass--radius relation, which depends sensitively on the internal stellar structure through the equation of state of dense nuclear matter, certain neutron-star properties obey approximately universal relations that exhibit only weak dependence on the equation of state. In this paper, we explore new universal relations for magnetized neutron stars. We focus on a purely poloidal dipolar magnetic-field configuration and treat the effects of the magnetic field perturbatively to second order in the field strength. After appropriately normalizing the relevant quantities by the stellar mass and the magnetic-field strength at the pole, we first identify an exact universal relation between the magnetic dipole moment and stellar compactness. We then find approximate universal relations among the magnetic dipole moment, magnetically-induced stellar quadrupole moment, and ellipticity, with fractional variations due to the equation of state at the level of O(10%). We support these numerical findings with analytic estimates for Newtonian polytropes, which reproduce the observed behavior. Among the newly discovered relations, the one connecting the magnetic dipole moment and stellar quadrupole moment may be particularly useful for the analysis of gravitational-wave signals from binaries containing magnetized neutron stars.

</details>


### [108] [Multiscale probing of a Hernquist-type environmental black hole spacetime with the Sgr A* shadow and S2 orbital dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07229)
*Lai Zhao,Meirong Tang,Zheng-Wen Long,Zhaoyi Xu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用Hernquist型环境黑洞时空模型，结合黑洞阴影数据和两组S2恒星轨道数据，通过MCMC方法约束银心超大质量黑洞Sgr A*周围环境物质的分布参数，得到环境致密度的上界约束，并揭示了尺度参数α的双峰简并性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sgr A*为探测黑洞周围环境物质分布提供了独特机会，但现有的黑洞阴影和恒星轨道数据对环境物质分布的约束尚不充分，需要利用非真空爱因斯坦场方程精确解来描述黑洞与其周围物质的联合引力场，并对模型参数进行严格约束。

Method: 采用Hernquist型环境黑洞时空（以无量纲致密度C和特征尺度α描述环境效应），将黑洞阴影数据与两组S2恒星轨道数据（Do et al.和Gillessen et al.）相结合，利用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）方法对模型参数进行统计约束。

Result: 在95%置信上限下，仅阴影数据约束C<1.498×10^-1但对α无有效约束；两组S2数据分别给出C<5.239×10^-5和C<1.303×10^-4，α均呈现双峰结构；联合阴影与S2数据后，C的上界分别收紧至C<3.760×10^-5和C<1.073×10^-4。

Conclusion: 现有观测排除了环境晕的高致密配置，所得约束与典型物质晕的致密度范围一致；但α仍存在显著的双峰简并性，表明当前观测不足以唯一确定环境晕的径向分布，未来多恒星轨道的观测有望进一步揭示环境晕的径向结构。

Abstract: The supermassive black hole Sgr A* at the Galactic center provides a unique opportunity to probe the distribution of environmental matter around black holes. In this work, we adopt the Hernquist-type environmental black hole spacetime, a non-vacuum exact solution of the Einstein field equations, as its gravitational model to describe the joint gravitational field of the black hole and its surrounding matter, with environmental effects characterized by the dimensionless compactness $C$ and the characteristic scale $α$. We combine black hole shadow data with two sets of S2 star data provided by Do et al. and Gillessen et al., and constrain the model parameters using the Markov chain Monte Carlo method. At the 95\% credible upper limit, the shadow-only data constrain $C < 1.498\times10^{-1}$.but provide no effective constraint on $α$. The two S2 datasets yield $C<5.239\times10^{-5}$ and $C<1.303\times10^{-4}$, respectively, with $α$ exhibiting a bimodal structure in both cases. After combining the shadow and S2 star data, the $C$ upper limits are tightened to $C<3.760\times10^{-5}$ and $C<1.073\times10^{-4}$, respectively. These results indicate that current observations rule out highly compact configurations of the environmental halo, while the obtained constraints are consistent with the typical compactness range of matter halos. However, $α$ still exhibits a significant bimodal degeneracy, indicating that current observations are insufficient to uniquely determine the radial distribution of the environmental halo. Future observations of multiple stellar orbits may provide further insights into the radial structure of the environmental halo.

</details>


### [109] [Testing the spin-induced multipole moments of compact binary coalescences using the flexible theory-independent framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07237)
*Elise M. Sänger,Alessandra Buonanno,Ajit Kumar Mehta,Jan Steinhoff*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于柔性理论无关（FTI）框架，对致密天体的自旋感应四极矩和八极矩进行了检验。通过合成信号研究其可测量性，应用于LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA观测信号，并对下一代地面探测器（爱因斯坦望远镜和宇宙探测器）做出预测，预计可将约束精度提升两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无毛定理指出广义相对论中电中性黑洞的多极矩完全由其质量和自旋决定，但对中子星或奇异致密天体并不成立——它们的多极矩取决于物态方程和形成历史。通过引力波检验致密天体双星并合的本质，需要一种能够独立于特定理论模型来测量自旋感应多极矩的框架。

Method: 采用柔性理论无关（FTI）框架，这是一种参数化的引力波旋进检验方法，允许在通用对齐自旋频域波形模型中向引力波相位添加后牛顿系数偏差。利用合成信号研究自旋感应四极矩和八极矩的可测量性，再将该检验应用于LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA实际观测信号，最后对下一代地面探测器进行约束预测。

Result: 下一代地面探测器（爱因斯坦望远镜和宇宙探测器）将能够对自旋感应四极矩和八极矩分别施加O(10^-2)和O(10^-1)量级的严格约束，比当前约束精度提高两个数量级。

Conclusion: 下一代引力波探测器将显著提升对致密天体自旋感应多极矩的约束能力，从而在致密双星并合中检验黑洞的本质，为区分黑洞与奇异致密天体提供更严格的理论判据。

Abstract: According to the no-hair theorem, the multipole moments of an electrically neutral black hole in general relativity are entirely determined by its mass and spin. However, this is not in general true for compact objects: The multipole moments of neutron stars or exotic compact objects can depend on their formation history and internal processes, which are encoded in an equation of state. Furthermore, their spin-induced multipole moments differ from those of black holes of the same mass and spin, leaving an imprint on the dynamics and emitted gravitational waves of the binary. Gravitational waves can thus be used to test the nature of compact binary coalescences. Here, we present a test of the spin-induced quadrupole and octupole moments of compact objects based on the flexible theory-independent (FTI) framework. FTI is a parameterized inspiral test of general relativity which enables the addition of post-Newtonian coefficient deviations to the gravitational-wave phase of a generic aligned-spin frequency-domain waveform model. We use this test on synthetic signals to study the measurability of spin-induced quadrupole and octupole moments. Next, we apply the test to a subset of signals observed by the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA Collaboration. Lastly, we present forecasts for next-generation ground-based detectors, such as Einstein Telescope and Cosmic Explorer. Our estimates suggest that these detectors will be capable of placing stringent constraints on the spin-induced quadrupole and octupole moments of $\mathcal{O}(10^{-2})$ and $\mathcal{O}(10^{-1})$ respectively, which is two orders of magnitude tighter than current constraints, and thus on the nature of black holes in a compact binary coalescence.

</details>


### [110] [Inflation with Nieh-Yan-like terms in metric-affine gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07386)
*Ilaria Andrei,Christian Dioguardi,Damianos Iosifidis,Laur Järv,Antonio Racioppi,Margus Saal*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了度量-仿射引力框架下具有非最小耦合标量场的慢滚暴涨，通过引入推广的Nieh-Yan项（标量场与挠率和非度量矢量散度的耦合），求解连接场方程后得到等效的爱因斯坦框架表述。对于特定的耦合函数选择，发现有效Nieh-Yan耦合强度参数可以显著改善Palatini暴涨与观测的一致性，在四次和二次Jordan框架势下分别给出了具体的参数区间和CMB可检验预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Palatini引力框架下的非最小耦合暴涨在特定情形下（如四次势）与CMB观测存在张力，需要寻找推广方案来恢复模型与观测的兼容性，并为下一代CMB实验提供新的可检验预言。

Method: 在度量-仿射引力中引入标量场与广义类Nieh-Yan项的耦合，通过射影相干性假设并求解连接场方程来积分掉挠率和非度量性，得到爱因斯坦框架下的修正动能函数和势。对特定耦合函数（A=MP^2+ξφ^2，Ci=ξiφ，V∝φ^k）进行数值慢滚计算，并与Planck、BICEP/Keck、ACT和SPT的CMB数据比较。

Result: 在大正有效Nieh-Yan耦合极限下，正则场满足χ~φ²，Jordan框架场值变为亚Planck尺度。四次势情形下，当ξ̄≲10⁴时张标比处于下一代CMB实验可达范围内；二次势情形下，10⁻²≲ξ̄≲10²可修复η问题并给出可行的预言。负耦合区间无法改善标准Palatini暴涨，但可产生不同且可检验的预言。

Conclusion: 推广的类Nieh-Yan耦合为Palatini暴涨与观测之间的兼容性提供了一种自然的解决方案，尤其对四次势和二次势模型给出了由耦合参数调控的、可供未来CMB实验检验的独特预言。

Abstract: We study single-field slow-roll inflation in metric-affine gravity with a scalar field non-minimally coupled to the non-Riemannian Ricci scalar and to the divergences of the torsion and nonmetricity vectors, a structure that generalizes the well-known Nieh-Yan term. By imposing projective coherence of the matter sector and solving the connection field equations, we integrate out torsion and nonmetricity and obtain an equivalent Einstein-frame formulation in which the metric-affine couplings are encoded in a modified kinetic function and potential. For the choice of coupling functions $\mathcal{A}(φ) = M_P^2 + ξφ^2$ to the non-Riemannian Ricci scalar, $\mathcal{C}_i(φ) = ξ_i φ$ to the Nieh-Yan-like terms and a monomial Jordan-frame potential $\mathcal{V} \propto φ^k$, we show that in the limit of a large positive effective Nieh-Yan-like coupling $\barξ$ the canonical field satisfies $χ\sim φ^2$, the Jordan-frame field values during inflation become sub-Planckian, and the Einstein-frame potential reduces to $U \sim χ^{k/2}$. We compute the slow-roll predictions numerically for quartic and quadratic Jordan-frame potentials and compare them with the current CMB constraints from Planck, BICEP/Keck, ACT, and SPT. We find that intermediate values of $\barξ$ can restore the compatibility of non-minimally coupled Palatini inflation with observations: in the quartic case, the model predicts a tensor-to-scalar ratio within reach of next-generation CMB experiments for $\barξ\lesssim10^4$, while in the quadratic case the coupling cures the $η$-problem arising for $ξ\gtrsim 10^{-2}$ and yields viable predictions for $10^{-2}\lesssim\barξ\lesssim 10^2$. In the negative $\barξ$ regime, the model does not improve upon standard Palatini inflation, though it can still produce distinct, testable predictions.

</details>


### [111] [Oscillations of Dissipative Neutron Stars: The Impact of Hyperonic Reaction Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07311)
*Suprovo Ghosh,Alexander Haber,Nils Andersson,Andrew Rhys Counsell*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了双中子星并合过程中有限化学反应速率对恒星振荡谱的效应，特别是在含超子物质的中子星中，通过将复杂频率依赖的动力学声速引入相对论扰动方程，展示了有限反应速率如何产生体粘滞耗散，影响f模和g模的性质，并在潮汐响应中引起潮汐滞后，为将粘滞耗散纳入引力波模型提供了框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在双中子星吸气过程中，潮汐激发恒星振荡模式是探测超核密度下致密物质组成的强大探针。然而，化学平衡在恒星微扰计算中常被忽略。以往计算通常假设瞬时化学平衡或完全不平衡（冻结组成），但实际反应速率可能是有限的。若化学平衡时标与振荡时标相近，粘滞效应会阻尼模式；若反应过快，某些模式会因恢复力消失而完全消失。因此需要研究有限反应速率对含超子物质中子星振荡谱的影响。

Method: 作者计算了主导的非轻子弱相互作用反应速率，并透过一个复值、频率依赖的动力学声速将其纳入相对论扰动方程。他们在恒星微扰中匹配近区边界条件来研究潮汐响应，从而展示粘滞耗散如何引起潮汐滞后。

Result: 结果显示，有限反应速率效应自然地表现为体粘滞耗散，改变了基本（f）模和重力（g）模的性质；在潮汐响应中，粘滞耗散导致潮汐滞后现象。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个将微观反应速率和相应体粘滞系数与致密双星潮汐动力学相联系的统一框架，是将粘滞耗散纳入双中子星吸气引力波模型的一步推进。

Abstract: Tidal excitations of stellar oscillation modes during binary neutron-star inspirals offer a powerful probe of the composition of dense matter at supranuclear densities. Chemical equilibration plays a crucial, but often neglected, role in stellar perturbation calculations. If the chemical equilibration timescale is comparable to the oscillation timescale, then viscous effects can damp the modes. If the reactions are fast, some modes can completely disappear since their restoring force vanishes. Typically, these calculations, however, assume either instantaneous chemical equilibrium or no equilibration (frozen composition). Motivated by this, we investigate the effects of finite reaction rates on the oscillation spectrum of neutron stars containing hyperonic matter. We calculate the dominant non-leptonic weak interaction rates and incorporate them into the relativistic perturbation equations through a complex, frequency-dependent dynamical sound speed. We show that finite-rate effects naturally manifest as bulk-viscous dissipation, modifying the properties of both the fundamental ($f$) and gravity ($g$) modes. We further examine the impact on the tidal response by matching stellar perturbations to near-zone boundary conditions, demonstrating how viscous dissipation gives rise to a tidal lag. These results provide a consistent framework connecting microscopic reaction rates and the resulting bulk viscosity to the tidal dynamics of compact binaries, and represent a step towards incorporating viscous dissipation into gravitational-wave models of binary neutron-star inspirals.

</details>


### [112] [Spotlight searches for continuous gravitational waves triggered on radiometer candidates in LIGO O4a data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07312)
*Alan M. Knee,Keith Riles,Ling Sun*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本研究对O4a观测运行的562个亚阈值连续引力波候选体进行了后续搜索，利用F统计量与隐马尔可夫模型结合的流水线，最终发现21个通过所有仪器否决的离群值，但在O4b数据中均未获得显著重现，因此未检测到令人信服的连续引力波信号。搜索在20–1726 Hz频段对孤立中子星达到h0~(0.63–6.3)×10^-25、对长周期双星系统中子星达到h0~(0.82–7.1)×10^-25的95%检测效率灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA O4a观测运行中的全天空全频率定向辐射计分析所识别的亚阈值连续引力波候选体进行后续监测，以确认是否存在真正的引力波信号而非噪声波动。

Method: 对562个候选体（每个包含1/32 Hz窄频带和约13平方度天区像素）进行参数空间搜索，使用基于F统计量的匹配滤波并结合隐马尔可夫模型以追踪可能发生频率漂移的连续引力波信号。所有候选体通过统计显著性与仪器否决过程筛选，并在O4b数据中进行独立验证。

Result: 获得21个误警概率低于5%且通过所有仪器否决的离群值，但在O4b数据中均未以可比的统计显著性重现，未检测到令人信服的连续引力波信号。通过注入模拟信号进行灵敏度估计，在20–1726 Hz频段内对孤立中子星达到95%检测效率的应变振幅范围为h0~(0.63–6.3)×10^-25，对长周期(>1年)双星系统中子星为h0~(0.82–7.1)×10^-25。

Conclusion: 在O4a数据中未发现可靠的连续引力波信号；观测结果对连续引力波搜索灵敏度提供了新的约束，灵敏度范围受探测器噪声频率依赖性的强烈影响尤其是低频区域。

Abstract: We report on a follow-up search for continuous gravitational waves (CWs) targeting sub-threshold candidates from the O4a LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA (LVK) all-sky all-frequency (ASAF) directed radiometer analysis. We analyze a total of $562$ ASAF candidates using Advanced LIGO data from the first part of the fourth LVK observing run. Each candidate comprises a narrow $1/32$ Hz frequency band paired with a ${\sim}13$ deg$^2$ sky pixel, defining the search parameter space for our analysis. Our search pipeline leverages the $F$-statistic matched filter equipped with a hidden Markov model to enable tracking of a potentially frequency-wandering CW signal. Of the 562 initial candidates, we obtain $21$ outliers with false alarm probability less than $5\%$ that survive all instrumental vetoes. None of the outliers are recovered at comparable statistical significance in the second part of the fourth observing run, from which we conclude that no convincing CW signals are detected. We estimate the sensitivity of our analysis by recovering simulated signals added to real detector data. Across the $20\text{--}1726$ Hz frequency band, our search achieves $95\%$ detection efficiency for strain amplitudes in the range of $h_0\sim (0.63\text{--}6.3)\times10^{-25}$ with respect to isolated neutron stars (NSs), and $h_0\sim (0.82\text{--}7.1)\times10^{-25}$ with respect to NSs in long-period ($>1$ yr) binary systems. The large range in sensitivity is due to the strong frequency dependence of detector noise, particularly at low frequencies.

</details>


### [113] [Gravitational caloric theory: From early dark energy to a wide variety of gravitational phenomena](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07318)
*S. X. Tian*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了引力量热理论（GCT），通过在广义相对论中引入一个由流体作为源且与流体非最小耦合的矢量场S_μ，设计了三种宇宙学应用：利用总流体状态方程触发早期暗能量（EDE）以解决巧合问题；通过Λ-抵消解实现自我调节机制以应对旧宇宙学常数问题（但存在无法进入标准热大爆炸阶段的局限）；以及从EDE相图的无穷远临界点启发提出的"早期静态热宇宙"作为暴胀的替代方案，用以解决视界问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在解决三个核心宇宙学问题：暗能量的巧合问题（通过EDE机制）、旧宇宙学常数问题（通过自调节机制）、以及暴胀框架所面临的视界问题（通过早期静态热宇宙方案）。GCT为该目标提供统一的理论框架。

Method: 作者将GCT背景演化方程重构为二维动力系统，通过庞加莱紧化探测系统的全局结构，分析无穷远临界点在相图中的角色。基于此分析，分别构造了EDE、Λ-抵消（自调节）和早期静态热宇宙三种宇宙学解决方案。

Result: EDE方案成功实现；Λ-抵消方案实现了自我调节但无法完整进入标准热大爆炸阶段，不能被视为完整的自调节解决方案；早期静态热宇宙方案通过S_μ抵消普通热气体的引力效应，产生准静态膨胀和递减的共动哈勃半径，可解决视界问题，作为暴胀的替代方案。

Conclusion: GCT为多重宇宙学问题提供了统一的理论框架，涵盖EDE、自调节机制和视界问题解决等多个方向。论文为后续在宇宙学、引力波和致密天体等领域的研究开辟了多条新路径。

Abstract: Gravitational caloric theory (GCT) extends general relativity by introducing a vector field $S_μ$ that is sourced by and non-minimally coupled to the fluid sector, while preserving covariant conservation of the standard fluid energy-momentum tensor. The initial motivation is to trigger early dark energy (EDE) using the total fluid equation of state that encodes the cosmic radiation-matter transition, thereby addressing the associated coincidence problem. The EDE dynamics are analyzed in detail by recasting the background evolution equations as a two-dimensional dynamical system. Poincaré compactification probes the global structure of this system and illustrates the role of critical points at infinity in shaping the phase portrait. There are two cosmological applications beyond EDE, both belonging to a broader family of energy-cancelling solutions in which conventional energy components (e.g., the cosmological constant $Λ$ or an ideal fluid) preferentially excite $S_μ$ rather than sourcing spacetime curvature. First, the $Λ$-cancelling solution corresponds to the so-called self-tuning mechanism proposed to address the old cosmological constant problem. A major limitation is that our present realization cannot enter the standard hot Big Bang phase and therefore should not be regarded as a complete self-tuning solution. Second, inspired by a nontrivial critical point at infinity in the EDE phase portrait, we propose an \textit{Early Static Hot Universe} in which $S_μ$ offsets the gravitational effect of ordinary hot gas, yielding quasi-static expansion with a decreasing comoving Hubble radius that can address the horizon problem. This scenario offers an alternative to inflation. This paper opens several avenues for further investigation of GCT, including cosmology, gravitational waves, and compact objects. (Abstract abridged to meet arXiv limits.)

</details>
